# Video Prompts: The Psychology of a Woman Who Chooses Herself

Visual Style Profile: Elara Cinematic. 359 clips, one per beat. Each prompt works on its own for text-to-video.
Clip length matches the voiceover at about 3 words per second. Generate at 16:9, 24fps, with no audio and no on-screen text; add the voiceover, music and captions in editing.

---
## Segment 1

### Clip 1 (3s)
**Script:** "Choose yourself. Not once. Not when it's convenient."

**Video prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic video clip, 3 seconds, 16:9, 24fps, 35mm film look with fine grain, modern coming-of-age drama aesthetic. Subject: a woman in her mid-twenties with long dark-brown wavy hair, warm olive skin, natural makeup and small gold hoop earrings, wearing a cream oversized knit sweater. Action: She stands at the balcony railing, eyes closed, taking a slow deep breath, moving naturally and unhurried, a candid unposed moment. Setting: a Milan apartment balcony overlooking terracotta rooftops at golden hour. Lighting: low golden-hour sun backlighting her, warm rim light on her hair. Camera: close-up on her face, 85mm lens, very shallow focus; very slow push-in on her face with a soft rack focus. Ambient motion: dust motes drifting in the warm light, a light breeze moving her hair and clothing. Mood: quiet resolve. Warm amber and honey tones against teal and navy shadows, muted soft-contrast orange-and-teal grade, shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh, realistic skin and hair detail, natural physics, no sudden cuts, no warping, no morphing faces, no speaking to camera, no text, no logos, no watermark.
